# Graph Report - bybloshq  (2026-08-23)

## Corpus Check
- 5482 files · ~5,134,396 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 65463 nodes · 87061 edges · 4234 communities (3974 shown, 260 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1955 edges (avg confidence: 0.86)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `8e694505`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
